## `AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Hydrostatic Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Nonwoven Fabric Mass-per-Unit-Area & Filtration Efficiency Lab
- **Intro:** Hydrostatic Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Nonwoven Fabric Mass-per-Unit-Area & Filtration Efficiency Lab — Pre-Start Environmental and Equipment Verification: Temperature: 23±2°C; RH: 50±5%; Voltage: 230 VAC ±10%; Ground resistance ≤ 1 Ω; Vibration amplitude ≤ 0.05 mm/s.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 23±2°C; RH: 50±5%; Voltage: 230 VAC ±10%; Ground resistance ≤ 1 Ω; Vibration amplitude ≤ 0.05 mm/s.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9073-1:2023`, `ISO 29463-1:2017`, `ISO 5725-1:2023`, `ANSI/ASME B40.100-2020 Pressure gauges and gauge attachments`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-1399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
